📦Info

🛠️Configuration files

The plugin generates the following configuration files, some of which will only be generated after you first use this feature.

datas: The location for storing plugin data files. It will only be generated without using a database. Do not modify any content here.

items: The location for storing saved item files.

languages: The location for storing language files. You can set the language file used by the plugin through the config-files.language option in the config.yml file. You can customize various messages within the plugin game through language files. For per player language, or send action bar/title/boss bar/sound, please view this page.

menus/common_menus: The location for storing common menu configuration files.

menus/shop_menu_templates: The location for storing shop menu template files.

ui: Shared graphical style files (classic.yml, bedrock.yml and custom presets). Select them with menu.ui.style or menu ui.style; see Menu Configuration & Inheritance.

menus/buy_more_menus: The location for storing buy more menu files.

menus/favourite_menus: The location for storing favourite menu files.

menus/search_menus: The location for storing search menu files.

menus/item_sell_menus: The location for storing item sell menu files.

Many people seem to be confused about one thing: what you open through the /shop command is a menu, not a shop. If you want to set the slot for items in the shop menu, you should set it in the configuration file of the menu.

shops: The location for storing shop configuration files.

placeholders/random_placeholders: The loaction for storing random placeholder configuration files.

placeholders/custom_placeholders: The location for storing custom placeholder configuration files.

placeholders/conditional_placeholders: The location for storing conditional placeholder configuration files.

sell_sticks: The location for storing sell stick configuration files.

config.yml file: The location for main common settings for plugins.

generated-item-format.yml file: When using the /shop generateitemformat command, we will parse the item you are holding into an ItemFormat and store the parsed ItemFormat content in this file.

minecraft-resources/<version>/: Downloaded Minecraft data, textures and language caches used by automatic menu icons and localized item names. These files are prepared automatically.

XX.txt file: The transaction record file generated by the Log Transaction feature is named using the log-transaction.file option in the config.yml file.

Legacy top-level placeholder and specialized menu folders are automatically moved into placeholders/ and menus/ when the plugin starts. If both locations contain the same file, the new file is used and the old file is preserved with a console warning for manual merging. Existing common menu files and custom subfolders in menus/ are moved into menus/common_menus/.

For Paper component ItemFormat examples and save/rebuild checks, see Component Format and Testing Paper Components.

Config.yml file content

It is recommend that you view this file at GitHub, becuase Wiki's config.yml maybe not latest. Click here to view this file on Github.

# UltimateShop by @PQguanfang
#
# Read the Wiki: ultimateshop.superiormc.cn

# Some options require restart the server to make effect.

debug: false

config-files:
language: en_US
# Premium version only.
per-player-language: true
force-parse-mini-message: true

# Support value: COMPONENT or LEGACY
# COMPONENT mode requires Paper 1.21.5+ and UltimateShop Premium.
debuild-item-method: 'LEGACY'

# Support value: DEFAULT or ITEMBRIDGE
hook-item-method: 'DEFAULT'

# By default, we will auto force display fail message to make sure player know whether they have successfully clicked the product button.
# If set to false, we will not force display fail message and you can also use hide-message option in shop configs to hide the fail messages.
force-display-fail-message: false

cache:
# If you are facing multi servers sync issue, try greater this value.
load-delay: 7
reset-task:
# Reset deadlines are rounded forward into this many seconds so nearby entries run together.
# Set to 0 to disable deadline merging. Maximum value: 3600.
merge-seconds: 1
# Maximum number of due reset entries processed in one server tick. Maximum value: 10000.
max-tasks-per-tick: 100

sell:
# Support Value: Bukkit or ItemFormat.
# For each product, you can add match-item section to make custom sell match method, for more info, please view Wiki.
sell-method: Bukkit
# Only support ItemFormat sell method.
item-format:
require-same-key: false
ignore-key:
- 'lore'
- 'damage'
- 'enchants'
- 'tool.damage-per-block'
- 'nbt.string.CustomNBTKey'
auto-hide-sell-all-message: true
sell-all:
ignore-items:
# Format: Shop ID;;Item ID
- 'hideshop;;A'
# PREMIUM version only.
sell-stick:
# The min value here is 5, if you set value less than 5, we will auto set the cooldown to 5 tick.
# In ticks.
cooldown: 5
# Support value: LEFT, RIGHT and LEFT;;RIGHT
# Do not change this value unless you are know what you are doing!
click-type: RIGHT
# PREMIUM version only.
sell-chest:
enabled: true
debug: false
period-ticks: 600
batch-count: 5
send-sell-message: true
price-empty: '{lang}'
hologram:
enabled: true
# Support value: DecentHolograms, CMI, FancyHolograms
plugin: 'DecentHolograms'
max-amount: -1
# Premium version only.
shulker-box-sell: true
# Premium version only
multiplier:
enabled: false
display-original-price: true
black-dynamic-price: true
black-shops:
- 'example'
# Support value: MAX, STACK
# MAX mode: will use the maximum value as the result
# STACK mode: As long as the player meets the conditions, it will be stacked and multiplied.
mode: STACK
value:
default: 1
rich: 0.9
vip: 1.1
value-conditions:
# Tax
rich:
1:
type: placeholder
placeholder: '%vault_eco_balance%'
rule: '>='
value: 50000
# Bonus for VIP
vip:
1:
type: permission
permission: 'group.vip'
# Item-aware modifiers are a separate global sell feature.
# Modifiers are applied in configuration order and merged into the final multiplier.
price-modifier:
item-sell-menu:
# Products with `price-modifier: true` open this item sell menu when a sell action is chosen.
enabled: true
menu: item-sell
durability:
type: durability
enabled: true
# 1 means losing 1% durability deducts 1% from the price.
deduction-coefficient: 1
minimum-multiplier: 0.1
# You can use minimum-price instead of, or together with, minimum-multiplier.
# minimum-price: 1
lore:
type: lore
enabled: false
# SET uses the captured number as the price; MULTIPLY multiplies the current price by it.
# Invalid operations are reported and the modifier is ignored.
operation: SET
# The default pattern matches lore such as: Item Value: 500
pattern: 'Item Value[：:]\s*([+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+))'
value-group: 1
strip-color: true
case-sensitive: true
# Values outside this inclusive range, or longer than maximum-number-length, are ignored.
minimum-value: 0
maximum-value: 1000000
maximum-number-length: 64
nbt:
type: nbt
enabled: false
# Requires the NBTAPI plugin. Nested keys can use dot notation.
key: item_value
# AUTO, BYTE, SHORT, INT, LONG, FLOAT, DOUBLE or STRING.
value-type: AUTO
# SET uses the NBT value as the price; MULTIPLY multiplies the current price by it.
# Invalid operations are reported and the modifier is ignored.
operation: SET
# Values outside this inclusive range, or longer than maximum-number-length, are ignored.
minimum-value: 0
maximum-value: 1000000
maximum-number-length: 64
mythic-changer:
type: match_item
enabled: false
# MAX, MIN or STACK. STACK applies matching rules in configuration order.
mode: STACK
rules:
named-item:
# ADD or MULTIPLY. Invalid operations are reported and this rule is ignored.
operation: MULTIPLY
# Supports math expressions and PlaceholderAPI. Invalid expressions are reported and ignored.
value: 1.2
match-item:
has-name: true
special-lore:
operation: ADD
# ADD adds this value to the price multiplier: 1 + 0.1 = 1.1.
value: 0.1
match-item:
contains-lore:
- 'Special'

give-item:
# Support value: BUKKIT, SMART
# SMART will cost more server performance but will follow the vanilla max stack to give player item, also support check full.
give-method: SMART
# Only support SMART give method.
check-full: true

# 0 selects the built-in format for your Minecraft version; override for a different client.
pack-format: 0

# Set -1 to disable.

# Decimal places used by math results. Applying a multiplier also counts as a math calculation.

format:
enabled: true
decimal: "#,##0.00##########"
integer: "#,##0"
strip-trailing-zeros:
enabled: true

# Premium version only.
log-transaction:
# It will cost extra performance cost.
enabled: false
# file | database — database requires database.enabled: true
storage: file
# Logs are kept in memory and flushed in batches for both file and database storage.
cache:
# How often pending logs are written, in ticks (20 ticks = 1 second).
flush-period-ticks: 200
# Flush early once this many pending logs have accumulated.
max-batch-size: 500
# Used when storage is file. If set to empty value, we will just print the log into console.
file: 'log.txt'
format: '{time} | {player} | {shop} | {buy-or-sell} | {item-name} x{amount} | {price} | Price Multiplier: x{multiplier}'
time-format: "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"

display-item:
# Require Paper 1.17.1+ version.
auto-translate-item-name: true
# Require Paper 1.21.9+ version, only supports Java players.
auto-use-sprite-item-name: true
# If set to true, if your product default amount is 10, and you purchase it x64 in one time, we will display 640 as result in amount placeholder.
calculate-amount: true
auto-set-first-product: true
# @+lower case means conditional lore, do not remove them here.
# Otherwise, the line without this will always display it.
add-lore:
- '{lang}'

placeholder:
auto-settings:
# If enabled, we will auto add conditional placeholder at all price amount.
# This can avoid the need to add the discount option in the amount options for each price.
add-conditional-in-all-price-amount:
enabled: false
buy-placeholder: buy
sell-placeholder: sell
black-dynamic-price: true
black-shops:
- 'example'
# We will try adds dynamic up or down symbol for all dynamic price placeholder.
# Please note that in your dynamic price amount option, first number must be the base price.
# If you made the dynamic price according to the Wiki's instructions, then there is no problem at all.
add-status-in-dynamic-price-placeholder:
enabled: true
# If enabled, we will try change {amount} in price placeholder option to the value you set here.
change-amount-in-all-price-placeholder:
enabled: false
replace-value: '%formatter_number_format_{amount}%'
# Premium version only
compare:
up: '{lang}'
down: '{lang}'
same: '{lang}'
cron:
format: "{lang}"
data:
# If your server never use dynamic value in prices or similar things, you can set this option to false.
# This will improve little plugin performance.
can-used-in-amount: true
refresh:
format: "{lang}"
never: "{lang}"
# Premium version only
next:
with-day-format: "{lang}"
without-day-format: "{lang}"
never: "{lang}"
price:
split-symbol-any: '{lang}'
split-symbol-all: '{lang}'
replace-new-line-symbol: '{lang}'
unknown: "{lang}"
unknown-price-type: "{lang}"
empty: "{lang}"
filter-for-one-line: ''
item-auto-use-sprite: true
click:
# If enabled, {buy-click} and {sell-stick} will display different value according to product status.
# But, it will maybe make server lag if you are running big server and have many products in your shop.
enabled: false
buy: '{lang}'
sell: '{lang}'
buy-with-no-sell: '{lang}'
sell-with-no-buy: '{lang}'
buy-max-limit-player: '{lang}'
buy-max-limit-server: '{lang}'
sell-max-limit-player: '{lang}'
sell-max-limit-server: '{lang}'
buy-price-not-enough: '{lang}'
sell-price-not-enough: '{lang}'
error: '{lang}'
buy-condition-not-meet: '{lang}'
sell-condition-not-meet: '{lang}'
# Premium version only.
sell-stick:
infinite: "{lang}"

database:
enabled: false
jdbc-url: "jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/ultimateshop?useSSL=false&autoReconnect=true"
properties:
user: root
password: 123456

# Premium version only.
bungeecord-sync:
enabled: false

prices:
example:
economy-plugin: Vault
amount: 200
placeholder: '{amount} Coins'
mmoitems-example:
hook-plugin: MMOItems
hook-item: AXE;;TEST_AXE
amount: 1
placeholder: '{amount} Mythic Axe'

conditions:
products-key: 'products-conditions'
buy-prices-key: 'buy-prices-conditions'
sell-prices-key: 'sell-prices-conditions'
display-item-key: 'display-item-conditions'

time-offset:
enabled: false
offset-hours: 0
offset-minutes: 0
offset-seconds: 0

auto-save:
enabled: true
hide-message: false
period-tick: 6000

bypass-plugin-check:
- 'PluginNameHere'

📦Info

📊Data

Data Type

UltimateShop has 2 data types, they are: Player Data and Server Data.

Player data saves player self data, like each product buy times and sell times, cooldown times, and other things.

Server data saved server total product buy times, sell times and so on. Random placeholder's data will also saved in server data.

Save

For player data, we will auto save player data into database when player leave the server.

For server data, we will auto save server data when you are trying to stop the server normally. (Not directly close the console window, you need use /stop command in server)

Data not saved will be lost after server crash, so enable auto save feature if you don't want this.

Auto Save

You can use auto save feature so that plugin can store plugin data periodically to avoid data loss due to server crashes. It is not recommended to store at a high frequency, as this can cause server lag. You can find the following content in config.yml to set this feature:

Copy

auto-save:
enabled: true
hide-message: false
period-tick: 6000 # In ticks, 20 ticks = 1 second.

Database

You can find the following content in config.yml to set this feature:

Database

JDBC URL Example

JDBC Class

MySQL

jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/ultimateshop

com.mysql.cj.jdbc.Driver

MariaDB (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:mariadb://localhost:3306/ultimateshop

org.mariadb.jdbc.Driver

PostgreSQL (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/ultimateshop

org.postgresql.Driver

SQLLite (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:sqlite:plugins/UltimateShop/data.db

org.sqlite.JDBC

h2 (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:h2:file:./plugins/UltimateShop/data

org.h2.Driver

📦Info

❓FAQ

Q: Player don't cost money when buy product.

A: That is simply because you give self * permission so plugin will bypass price check for you. For common player, it will work correctly.

Q: My item can not be sold in shop, but I think they are same item.

A: No, actually they have small different, but you dind't find them. The simplest way is to change the sell-methods option in config.yml from Bukkit to ItemFormat.

Q: Does the /shop command has permission?

A: Use /shop can directly open a menu called main. This is a feature called Auto Open, and you can disable it at config.yml file with menu.auto-open.enabled option. If you only want some players to be exposed to this command, you can set conditions for the menu so that only players who meet the specified conditions can open the menu. For more info, please view Menus page.

Q: Why it say condition not meet when open daily example menu?

A: That menu already has a condition, which you can find in menus/shop_menu_templates/daily-shop-menu.yml.

Q: Why can I only sell 64x (or other amunt) items at once?

A: The person asking this question may never know a truth: before using the plugin, you should be familiar with the config.yml file. In config.yml, there is an option called sell.max-amount, and I want you to know its purpose by looking at its name.

Q: How to change the max amount limit of buy more menu?

A: There is a option called menu.buy-more.<buy more menu name>.max-amount option in config.yml file, or buy-more-menu.max-amount option in your product configs if you are setting up a separate buy more menu for a certain product.

Q: What does start-apply mean?

A: This means which times this price will apply. If you set it to 5, price will apply after player buy or sell this product 5 times.

This option will only work for ANY or ALL price-mode.

Q: Item dupe when quickly click item outside menu.

A: Some other plugin or mod lead to this problem. Try enable ignore-click-outside option jn config.yml file.

Q: What is different from free version and premium version?

A: Check this page for more info.

Q: Can I set different add lore for each product?

A: Yes, in Shop page we have told you that add-lore also works in each product configs!

Q: Shop menu can not open after use once!

A: Make sure your config.yml is latest format, if not, update it. Or, you are using menu cooldown system, disable it may fix.

Q: How can I translate item name in buy more menu and plugin message?

A: View Localized Item Name page.

Q: Why my item name is in Chinese?

A: Disable Localized Item Name feature.

Q: UltimateShop print Error:XXX message in console.

A: What I want to tell you is already put in the error message itself, like:

Error: Can not get prices section in your shop config!!

If there are no issues with the shop, there is no need to pay attention to it. The error message reported by the plugin is a prompt, and the plugin will automatically attempt to fix it once it detects this error.

Q: How to reset dynamic price?

A: View Dynamic Price page.

📦Info

⚙️Install

Install

Put the .jar file into your server's plugins folder.

Stop your server and then restart it. Cannot load plugins in any other way while the server is starting.

When updating plugins, please be sure to remove old versions.

Previously, you used the free version, but now to upgrade to the paid version, you only need to install the paid version on the server and remove the free version. The configuration file of the plugin does not require any changes.

When downgrading from a new game version to an old version on the server where the plugin is located, it is important to remove the items folder from the configuration file.

When using the Localized Item Name feature, it is important to note that when switching server game versions, the previously generated localized files need to be deleted.

UltimateShop is just a shop plugin and does not provide custom economy functionality. If you need a custom economy as your server economy, please find a suitable economy plugin yourself. Vault is not an economy plugin, it is just a dependency plugin for many economy plugins. After installing Vault on the server, it is also necessary to install the economy plugins that support it.

Default configs are just helping you understand the config framework, you need modify them that meet your needs.

📦Info

🚀Performance

UltimateShop is just a shop plugin that usually does not have too much impact on the server. It is usually a performance issue caused by your unreasonable settings or other plugins, such as:

Do not use ANY, ALL price/product mode unless necessary.

Different from CLASSIC_ANY, CLASSIC_ALL mode, ANY and ALL mode, the calculation for each buy or sell is independent. Only use the 2 mode when you set dynamic price or limit.

MMOItems/MythicMobs Item generation performance.

Both plugins are spend much server resource to generate item than vanilla items. If possible and your item is static item with no things like dynamic stat or level requirement, you can try use Save Item instead.

For suggestions below:
The change you need made are all exist in config.yml file.

Disable sell chest.

If you are not using sell chest feature, you can disable this feature to save serve performance.

Copy

sell:
sell-chest:
enabled: false

Set placeholder.click.enabled option to false.

This maybe save server performance.

Copy

click:
# If enabled, {buy-click} and {sell-stick} will display different value according to product status.
# But, it will maybe make server lag if you are running big server and have many products in your shop.
enabled: false

Set cooldown for menu system.

Recommend use when you are running big server, it will make player no longer quickly click and reopen shop menu to make sure UltimateShop not lag your server.

Do not enable auto update GUI feature.

Enabling these options will significantly increase server performance consumption, but buttons within the GUI will be able to automatically update.

In most cases, you just want the GUI to update and display the latest data when we reset buy times or sell times, so you can achieve this idea through use-times.auto-reset-mode option.

If you pursue ultimate plugin performance, it's best not to turn on this option either.

Use BUKKIT item give method.

This item give method has the best performance, but the cost is that there may be some issues with stacking items, and when the player's inventory is full, we can only throw the excess items on the ground instead of preventing the player from further trading.

Use Bukkit sell item match method.

This can save some server performance, but the drawback is that the items to be sold must be identical to the items in the shop, with no differences, even including anvil cost, enchants, etc. (Items with durability and enchantable items basically cannot be sold to the shop because of this)

Previous📊Data

Next❓FAQ

Last updated 4 months ago

Do not use ANY, ALL price/product mode unless necessary.

MMOItems/MythicMobs Item generation performance.

Disable sell chest.

Set placeholder.click.enabled option to false.

Set cooldown for menu system.

Do not enable auto update GUI feature.

Use BUKKIT item give method.

Use Bukkit sell item match method.

Copy

# Recommend use when you are running big server, it will make player no longer quickly click
# and reopen shop menu to make sure UltimateShop not lag your server.
# In ticks.
cooldown:
click: 6 # Default value is -1, set value greater than 5 is recommended.
reopen: 20 # Default value is -1, set value greater than 5 is recommended.

Copy

menu-update:
# Whether menu will refresh every buttons every 1 second.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in display item lore.
# But maybe lead to server lag if you have much online players, and they are all opening shop GUI.
circle-update: false
# Whether menu will refresh every buttons when click any of them.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in display item lore.
# But maybe lead to server lag if you have much online players, and they are all opening shop GUI.
click-update: false
# PREMIUM version only, if enabled, can update dynamic value used in GUI title.
title-update:
# Whether gui title will refresh every buttons every 1 second.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in menu title.
circle-update: false
# Whether gui title will refresh every buttons when click any of them.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in menu title.
click-update: true
resend-items-pack: false

Copy

auto-reset-mode: true

Copy

auto-reset-mode: false

Copy

give-item:
# Support value: BUKKIT, SMART
# SMART will cost more server performance but will follow the vanilla max stack to give player item, also support check full.
give-method: BUKKIT
# Only support SMART give method.
check-full: false

Copy

sell:
# Support Value: Bukkit or ItemFormat.
# For each product, you can add match-item section to make custom sell match method, for more info, please view Wiki.
sell-method: Bukkit

📦Info

✅Requirements

Java Version

Basic Requirement: Java16+

Java 17+ is recommended. Java17 and above versions are recommended, but plugins are compiled using Java16, so theoretically, you only need Java16 or higher versions.

Server Software

Server

Can work in your server

Can get offical support

Spigot

✅
Some features does not provide in Spigot servers

✅

Paper/Purpur

✅
Can provide subtle performance improvements.

✅

Folia

❓
Any problems only occurs in Folia servers may not be solved. Plugin is not designed for multi thread support.

❓

Folia's support is in the early testing stage and may be released in official versions or removed in the future. This support is not a guarantee.

Other softwares

❌

❌

Server Version

Version

Can work in your server

Can get offical support

Below 1.14

❌

❌

1.14 ~ 1.20.4

❓
The plugin can run on these versions, however, any issues caused on these versions will not be resolved.

❌

1.20.5 and above

✅
Using versions 1.21.5 and above is the most recommended, as you can enjoy the best features.

✅

If you encounter errors while using a certain version, please join our Discord feedback.

An economy plugin if you want to use custom currency

UltimateShop is just a shop plugin and does not provide custom economy functionality. If you need a custom economy as your server economy, please find a suitable economy plugin yourself. Vault is not an economy plugin, it is just a dependency plugin for many economy plugins. After installing Vault on the server, it is also necessary to install the economy plugins that support it.

🧭Menu Types

🔽Buy More Menus

Buy More selects a quantity for an already selected product. The quantity counts trade units; a unit can reward several physical items. Open it through a product's select-amount operation or a buy_more_menu action with shop/product context.

Inventory quantity files live in menus/buy_more_menus/. Bundled files are buy-more.yml, buy-more-buy.yml and buy-more-sell.yml. They are not standalone menus for /shop menu.

Select a menu and maximum

Global defaults in config.yml:

Copy

menu:
buy-more-menu:
not-open-when-invalid: true
display-item-max-stack: true
default:
menu: buy-more
max-amount: 693
only-buy:
menu: buy-more-buy
max-amount: 693
only-sell:
menu: buy-more-sell
max-amount: 693

Setting under menu.buy-more-menu

Purpose

default

Menu/maximum for products with both price directions

only-buy

Menu/maximum for products without sell prices

only-sell

Menu/maximum for products without buy prices

not-open-when-invalid

Prevent opening when no confirmation control is valid for the product

display-item-max-stack

Use an extended preview stack maximum of 99 on supported Minecraft 1.20.5+ runtimes

Shop settings.buy-more supplies the enablement default. Product buy-more and buy-more-menu can override it:

This is a fragment to add to an existing product. The selected quantity is clamped from 1 to its configured maximum. Preview capacity does not set that maximum.

The bundled product click binding is menu.click-event.select-amount: SHIFT_RIGHT. Set a click binding to NEVER to disable it. A buy_more_menu action accepts shop and item; Premium actions can additionally provide buy-more-menu.menu and buy-more-menu.max-amount for that opening. See Custom Click Event.

Inventory quantity example

Save this as a new file in menus/buy_more_menus/ and select its ID in the settings above:

The amount, preview and confirmation IDs must appear in layout. Ordinary buttons can supply fillers or navigation; use separate IDs for each control type. The example starts with quantity 1.

Quantity file reference

Key

Behavior

amount-items.<id>.display-item

Appearance of the quantity control

amount-items.<id>.add-amount

Positive/negative adjustment

amount-items.<id>.set-amount

Fixed value when add-amount is absent or zero

display-items

Ordered preview markers; takes priority over singular display-item

display-item

Alternative single preview marker

display-items-padding-item

Display Item Format used for unused preview space

confirm-items.<id>.display-item

Confirmation appearance; supports {amount}

confirm-items.<id>.click-action

Fixed operation such as buy, sell, buy-or-sell or sell-all; omitted uses the click's configured operation

confirm-items.<id>.modify-lore

Generate product lore for the selected quantity; default true

Control menu-visibility

Presentation filtering for a control

dialog.enabled

Allow native quantity input when Dialog is available

Preview stacks split the quantity across preview slots in order, using the effective display stack maximum. Unused space shows padding or air. Confirmation uses normal trade validation and the selected quantity; displaying a large preview does not grant items.

This file supports ordinary title, size, layout, buttons and lifecycle settings. It does not inherit its parent shop's settings.menu-settings or global graphical menu.ui. Its bedrock, arcmenu, ui and display-items-ui fields do not define alternative quantity screens.

Quantity selection in each presentation

Presentation

Quantity controls

Text configuration

Inventory

This file's amount buttons, previews and confirmations

This Buy More file

Bedrock Form

Text entry, followed by product information

Source shop menu bedrock.buy-or-sell, then global menu.bedrock.buy-or-sell

Native Dialog

Native amount input when this file enables Dialog; otherwise Inventory

Source shop menu dialog.buy-more, then global menu.dialog.buy-more

ArcMenu

Product details with quantity controls

Graphical settings and ArcMenu text

Form quantity entry belongs to the source shop flow; it does not render this file's slot controls. Dialog keeps action-specific menu/maximum overrides through input and confirmation. Global forced UI can override the normal Dialog selector.

ArcMenu's select-amount opens product details. Quantity adjustment requires Buy More, a selected quantity menu and an available quantity operation. Right-click defaults to select-amount; plus/minus and scrolling adjust quantity. Compatible ArcMenu versions also provide direct quantity text input. Inventory confirmation labels do not rename graphical or native information actions.

Default trade amounts

Before manual quantity selection, default-buy-amount and default-sell-amount apply independently. After selection, both directions use the chosen count. Generated {buy-amount} and {sell-amount} reflect the corresponding operation; an explicit display-item stack amount remains separate. See Default Trade Amounts.

Shop Menus · UI Modes · Display Item Add Lore

Select a menu and maximum

Inventory quantity example

Quantity file reference

Quantity selection in each presentation

Default trade amounts

Copy

items:
A:
buy-more: true
buy-more-menu:
menu: buy-more
max-amount: 128

Copy

title: 'Choose amount'
size: 27
layout:
- '000000000'
- '01a2b3c00'
- '000D0E000'
dialog:
enabled: false
amount-items:
'1':
display-item:
material: RED_WOOL
name: '&c-1'
add-amount: -1
'2':
display-item:
material: GREEN_WOOL
name: '&a+1'
add-amount: 1
'3':
display-item:
material: PAPER
name: '&eSet to 64'
set-amount: 64
display-items: [a, b, c]
display-items-padding-item:
material: GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE
name: ' '
confirm-items:
D:
display-item:
material: EMERALD
name: '&aBuy x{amount}'
click-action: buy
modify-lore: true
E:
display-item:
material: GOLD_INGOT
name: '&eSell x{amount}'
click-action: sell
modify-lore: true

🧭Menu Types

❤️Favourite Menus - Premium

Favourite menus save a player's chosen shop products and order. The list belongs to the Favourite menu ID, so actions adding products must target that same ID. Products retain their source shop prices and trade rules.

Save files in menus/favourite_menus/. Open the bundled menu with /shop menu favourite or an open_menu action. The folder assigns the type; Common-folder files can use menu-type: favourite (alias favorite) or the legacy menu.favourite-gui.menu list.

Add a product

The bundled add-favourite operation uses SWAP_OFFHAND (F). Its custom action targets the bundled favourite list:

Copy

type: add_favourite
menu: favourite
shop: '{shop}'
item: '{item}'

Use this inside an action list with product context. For a custom list such as my-favourites, change menu to that ID in the adding action. Products must allow favourites. Native and graphical information/extra controls can expose the same configured operation; a label alone does not create an action binding.

Inventory example

Save as menus/favourite_menus/my-favourites.yml:

Copy

title: 'Favourites'
content: 'Your saved products.'
size: 27
layout:
- '000000000'
- '011111110'
- '0000D0000'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
result-item: '1'
edit-mode-item:
D:
normal-display-item:
material: NAME_TAG
name: '&eEdit favourites'
editing-display-item:
material: WRITABLE_BOOK
name: '&aFinish editing'
empty-item:
display-item:
material: GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE
name: '&7Empty favourite slot'
result-lore:
- '&7Shop: {shop}'
- '&7Product: {product}'
editing-result-lore:
- '&eLeft: move forward'
- '&eRight: move backward'
- '&cDrop: remove'

Reload, add products to my-favourites, then open /shop menu my-favourites. The example selects Inventory in normal selection; global forced UI still applies.

Favourite configuration

Key

Purpose

result-item, result-items

Combined result markers; default 6 when neither is supplied

edit-mode-item.<id>.normal-display-item

Edit toggle appearance in normal mode

edit-mode-item.<id>.editing-display-item

Toggle appearance in editing mode

edit-mode-item.<id>.display-item

Fallback appearance when a variant is missing

edit-mode-item.<id>.slot

Zero-based fallback when the toggle ID is absent from layout

empty-item.display-item

Placeholder for an unfilled result slot

result-lore

Normal result instructions; {shop} and {product} identify the source

editing-result-lore

Editing instructions

Special control menu-visibility

Presentation visibility

Special control ui

ArcMenu placement and appearance

Use one resolved edit toggle. Root result markers determine the maximum displayed source entries, filled in saved order. Graphical pagination does not independently increase this capacity.

Edit-toggle text supports {editing}, {edit-mode}, {favourite-amount} and {favorite-amount}. Empty-slot text supports the amount aliases and {slot-index}, the one-based result position.

Normal and editing modes

Normal mode uses product click operations. The edit toggle switches modes without executing a trade.

Inventory input in editing mode

Operation

Left click

Move forward one position

Right click

Move backward one position

Drop / Control-Drop

Remove from this list

Removing a favourite does not remove player items or shop stock. Turn editing off to resume product operations. The saved list and order are shared across presentations.

Other presentations

Presentation

Normal mode

Editing mode

Bedrock Form

Product information Form

Product opens Forward/Backward/Remove/Back controls

Native Dialog

Product information/actions

Dedicated edit controls; empty result slots are omitted

ArcMenu

Product cards, operations and details

Forward/Backward/Remove card buttons; left/right/middle edit clicks, with middle removing

Form may display configured empty-slot buttons. ArcMenu needs ui.placement for the edit toggle and navigation, and ui.products.placement for result cards. Empty-slot graphical placement can be disabled.

Form editing text uses bedrock.favourite-edit with global menu.bedrock.favourite-edit fallback; Dialog uses dialog.favourite-edit and menu.dialog.favourite-edit. These contain title, content and buttons.forward/backward/remove/back; titles/content support {item-name}.

ArcMenu card edit labels default to the Form labels and can be overridden with ui.text.favourite-edit.forward/backward/remove. Adapt editing lore to each interface with presentation conditions.

Favourite does not render Ore graphics. Setting dialog.layout: ore uses native multi-action and generates no Ore assets for this type.

UI Modes · Custom Click Event · Graphical UI Layout

🧭Menu Types

🔲Common Menus

Common menus display configured buttons for navigation and custom actions. Save them in menus/common_menus/ and open them with /shop menu <id> or an open_menu action.

Create a navigation menu

Save as menus/common_menus/main.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Main Menu'
size: 9
layout:
- '000A0X000'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

Create the farming shop, run /shop reload, then /shop menu main. The example selects Inventory in normal selection; global menu.force-ui can override the switches.

Use type: shop_menu with shop to open a shop. Use type: open_menu with menu for another Common, Search, Favourite or Item Sell menu. A Common menu does not automatically populate shop products.

Common menu keys

Key

Purpose

title

Screen title

content

Shared description fallback

size, layout

Inventory size and entry IDs

buttons

Ordinary display items, actions and conditions

conditions

Conditions for opening the menu

open-actions, close-actions

Actions for menu lifecycle events

dynamic-layout

Per-player layout evaluation — Premium

dialog, bedrock, arcmenu

Presentation switches and supported native options

ui

Ore/ArcMenu style, layout and drawing settings

Button click conditions belong in buttons.<id>.conditions; button display conditions belong in buttons.<id>.display-conditions. Root conditions applies to the whole menu. See Layout & Buttons.

Use another presentation — Premium

To use native Dialog, replace the example's Dialog switch with:

Also enable global menu.dialog.enabled. For Ore, select dialog.layout: ore, configure graphical placements and distribute its generated pack. For ArcMenu, enable the global/menu ArcMenu switches and configure graphical placements with a compatible integration. Bedrock Form uses the global/menu Bedrock switches.

UI Modes explains priority, forced selection and supported clients. Graphical UI Layout explains styles and areas.

Custom command — Premium

Add this at menu root to register a command for the menu:

The command opens this menu for the player. Choose a name that does not conflict with an existing command.

Inventory refresh and title updates

Global menu.menu-update.circle-update refreshes item displays every second; click-update refreshes after clicks. Premium title updates use menu.title-update.enabled/circle-update/click-update and require PacketEvents. Full settings are listed in Inventory UI.

Typed files in the Common folder

The dedicated Search, Favourite and Item Sell folders classify their files automatically. Existing Common-folder files can declare root menu-type: search, favourite (alias favorite) or item-sell (alias item_sell). Search/Favourite IDs can also be classified by their global legacy menu lists.

Prefer dedicated folders for new typed menus. Their specialized controls and presentation support follow their declared type, even when stored in common_menus.

Shop Menus · Configuration & Inheritance

Copy

name: categories

🧭Menu Types

💰Item Sell Menus

Item Sell accepts real item stacks, previews their sell value and waits for an explicit confirmation. Closing without confirming returns the items. Use Sell All when deposits should instead be sold on close.

Save files in menus/item_sell_menus/. Open the bundled menu with /shop menu item-sell or an open_menu action. The folder assigns the menu type automatically; a file in common_menus can use root menu-type: item-sell (alias item_sell).

Create a deposit menu

Copy

title: 'Sell Items'
size: 54
max-sell-amount: -1
layout:
- '000000000'
- '0IIIIIII0'
- '0IIIIIII0'
- '0IIIIIII0'
- '000000000'
- '0000C0X00'
input-item: 'I'
confirm-item: 'C'
confirm-button:
display-item:
material: EMERALD
name: '&aConfirm Sale'
lore:
- '&7Price: &f{price}'
- '&7Original price: &f{original-price}'
- '&7Multiplier: &fx{multiplier}'
- '&7Sellable items: &f{item-amount}'
- '&7Unsellable items: &f{unsellable-amount}'
buttons:
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

Save as menus/item_sell_menus/my-sell.yml, reload, then open /shop menu my-sell. I marks deposit slots; C marks confirmation. Undefined 0 slots are empty but do not accept sale deposits.

Configuration reference

Key

Purpose

input-item

Primary input marker; default I

input-items

Additional input markers, combined with input-item

confirm-item

Confirmation marker; default C, can appear in multiple slots

confirm-button.display-item

Confirmation icon, name and lore

max-sell-amount

Maximum trade units sold across all inputs per confirmation; -1 is unlimited

Common title, layout, ordinary buttons, opening conditions and lifecycle actions are supported. Longer markers use the normal layout token rules. The built-in confirmation control uses confirm-button, separate from Buy More's confirm-items.

Price preview and confirmation

Confirmation placeholder

Value

{price}

Final reward with applicable sell multipliers and item-aware modifiers

{original-price}

Reward before those multipliers

{multiplier}

Combined effective multiplier

{item-amount}

Amount eligible to sell

{unsellable-amount}

Amount currently ineligible

The preview updates from deposited items. Confirming runs the normal product sell flow, including conditions, limits, events, actions and transaction logging. Unsold items remain available and are returned on close. Menu icons are not player sale stock.

max-sell-amount caps this menu's combined confirmation. It does not override product stock, conditions or other sell restrictions.

Route item-aware products here

Products using price-modifier: true need Item Sell so pricing can inspect the actual supplied stacks. Configure the target in config.yml:

For these products, the sell and sell-all operations route to that menu. The target ID must exist. Form/Dialog listings first expose product information so players can choose buying or selling; routed selling opens the physical Inventory. The product defines its modifiers, while the menu supplies input and confirmation.

Presentation support

Item Sell is Inventory-only for all clients. Its settings do not include Form, Dialog, Ore or ArcMenu layout, and it does not inherit graphical menu.ui. Global forced UI preserves this physical deposit interface.

Price Modifier · Sell Multiplier · Inventory UI

Create a deposit menu

Configuration reference

Price preview and confirmation

Route item-aware products here

Presentation support

Copy

sell:
price-modifier:
item-sell-menu:
enabled: true
menu: item-sell

🧭Menu Types

🔍Search Menus - Premium

Search finds shop products visible to the player by item and/or name. Inventory supports physical item filters and chat keyword input. Form, native Dialog and ArcMenu provide their own keyword flows while retaining source product prices and trade rules.

Store files in menus/search_menus/. Open the bundled screen with /shop menu search or an open_menu action. Files in this folder are automatically Search menus. Common-folder files can use menu-type: search or the legacy menu.search-gui.menu ID list in config.yml.

Inventory example

Save as menus/search_menus/my-search.yml:

Copy

title: 'Search'
content: 'Find a product.'
size: 27
layout:
- '000HNC000'
- '001111100'
- '066666660'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
input-item: '1'
result-item: '6'
action-items:
H:
action-type: input-name
display-item:
material: HOPPER
name: '&eEnter a name'
C:
action-type: clear-search
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClear'
state-items:
N:
empty-input:
display-item:
material: PAPER
name: '&7Waiting for input'
has-input:
display-item:
material: COMPASS
name: '&a{showing-amount}/{result-amount} results'
no-result-item:
slot: 22
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cNo results'
result-lore:
- '&7Shop: {shop}'
- '&7Product: {product}'

Reload, then open /shop menu my-search. In normal selection, this uses Inventory: deposit filter items in 1 slots, click H to enter a keyword, or C to clear. Global forced UI takes priority over the local switches.

Search configuration

Key

Purpose

input-item, input-items

Combined physical input markers; default 1 if none are configured

result-item

Result marker; default 6; occurrences set result capacity

action-items.<id>.action-type

input-name (alias search-name) or clear-search

action-items.<id>.display-item

Special action appearance

clear-search-item.<id>

Legacy action section; defaults to clear-search

state-items.<id>.empty-input.display-item

Status before input

state-items.<id>.has-input.display-item

Status after input

no-result-item.slot

Zero-based Inventory slot for no-match display

no-result-item.display-item

No-match appearance

result-lore

Extra result lore; {shop} and {product} refer to its source

Ordinary navigation belongs in buttons. Put special action/state IDs in root layout, reserve input/result markers, and use a valid no-result slot. Longer IDs follow Layout & Buttons.

Status items support {result-amount} (all matches), {showing-amount} (displayed matches), {input-amount} (physical input count) and {name-keyword}. These values belong to search status rendering rather than arbitrary menu text.

Keyword and item input

Inventory physical inputs filter products linked to those items. A keyword filters product/item names; text-only search works without deposited stacks. Name matching uses the shared localized item-name system.

Chat prompt keywords are global in config.yml:

cancel preserves the prior keyword; clear removes it. Bundled {lang} values use the configured language. Clear-search also returns physical Inventory input. Closing or navigating away returns remaining deposited stacks. Result/display icons are GUI items, not deposited stock.

Presentation behavior

Presentation

Input

Controls

Inventory

Physical items and chat keyword

Configured action, state and no-result items

Bedrock Form

Custom Form keyword input

Search button and ordinary navigation; no physical input/status slots

Native Dialog

Native keyword input

Search submission and result information; no physical input slots

ArcMenu

Chat keyword input

Graphical action/state/no-result controls and product cards

Form opens its home screen before keyword entry. Blank Form/Dialog keywords show no products. Input prefills the previous keyword; cancellation preserves it. Product information Back restores the search. Internal input submission/cancellation does not repeatedly run menu lifecycle actions; ordinary navigation leaves the flow.

Form labels use menu bedrock.search.* with global menu.bedrock.search.* fallback. Dialog uses dialog.search.* and global menu.dialog.search.*.

Graphical search

Search supports ArcMenu graphics. Configure ui.products.placement for results and ui.placement on ordinary and special controls. Root result-slot capacity still limits how many source results can be shown; graphical pagination does not expand that limit.

Search does not render Ore graphics or generate Ore assets. dialog.layout: ore selects native multi-action for this type. Presentation switches and forced selection follow UI Modes.

Favourite Menus · ArcMenu · Graphical UI Layout

Inventory example

Search configuration

Keyword and item input

Presentation behavior

Graphical search

Copy

menu:
prompt:
cancel-keyword: cancel
search-gui:
prompt:
clear-keyword: clear

🧭Menu Types

🛒Sell All Menu

Sell All is one global Inventory screen. Players deposit items and close the window to sell them. It has no explicit confirmation button. Item Sell provides price preview and confirmation when that flow is preferred.

Open the screen

Use /shop sellall with permission ultimateshop.sellall, or add this action to a menu button:

Copy

actions:
1:
type: sell_all_menu

Opening from Form, Dialog, Ore or ArcMenu still creates a physical Inventory.

Configure the screen

In config.yml:

Copy

menu:
sell-all:
size: 54
title: '{lang}'
black-slots: []

Key under menu.sell-all

Purpose

size

Slot count: 9, 18, 27, 36, 45 or 54

title

Screen title; {lang} uses the matching language override

black-slots

Zero-based slots that reject item placement, for example [0, 1, 2]

This screen has no separate file in menus/, no ordinary layout/button configuration and no independent menu IDs. Graphical styles and forced presentation selection do not change it.

Configure sale exclusions

Exclude shop products in config.yml using shop-id;;product-id:

This excludes that product reference from the Sell All flow. See product configuration for prices and sell requirements.

Sale on close

Closing scans eligible shop products and runs normal selling against deposited items. Product conditions, limits, exclusions and applicable multipliers remain in effect. Global sell.max-amount limits the sell engine; it is separate from Inventory capacity and Item Sell's max-sell-amount.

Unsold items return to the player, or drop when they cannot fit. An empty menu produces no reward. Products requiring item-aware price modifiers use the configured Item Sell flow.

Item Sell · Inventory UI

PreviousItem Sell Menus

NextUI Modes

Last updated 7 days ago

Open the screen

Configure the screen

Configure sale exclusions

Sale on close

Copy

sell:
sell-all:
ignore-items:
- 'farming;;A'

🧭Menu Types

🛍️Shop Menus

A shop file holds products, prices and trade rules. Its menu template holds layout, navigation buttons and presentation settings. Multiple shops can share one template.

Create a shop and template

Save this template as menus/shop_menu_templates/farming.yml:

Copy

title: '{shop-name}'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0X00'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

Save the shop as shops/farming.yml:

With Vault and a compatible economy installed, run /shop reload, then /shop menu farming. In normal Inventory selection, marker A displays shop product items.A, and X displays the template's Close button. Global menu.force-ui can override the local presentation switches.

settings.menu: farming selects the template ID; /shop menu farming opens the shop ID. These IDs can differ. Shop templates are used by shops rather than opened as standalone Common menus.

Override one shop's template

In the shop file:

Overrides merge recursively into the template; lists such as layout replace the full list. Global graphical defaults fill missing ui fields afterward. settings.menu-settings is also usable without a template when it supplies the complete screen definition. See Configuration & Inheritance.

Add products and navigation

Add products under shop items, then reference their IDs in the template's Inventory layout. Ordinary navigation buttons can live in template buttons or shop buttons. Keep IDs distinct across these sources.

Product clicks use menu.click-event and product overrides. Ordinary buttons use their action lists. Product pricing and rewards follow Products; graphical layout does not change trading rules.

Reuse a layout across shops

Use shop-items in the shop menu template to mark product slots. The four P slots below show the first four products under the shop's items section, from left to right. Spare slots stay empty if the shop has fewer products.

To place numbered positions independently, use several markers such as shop-items: ['A', 'B', 'C']. In the layout, A shows the first product, B the second and C the third. Product order follows the order in the shop file.

Use regular button and product IDs in other slots. For example, X can be a close button. Products still follow their display conditions and visibility settings. To override the template for one shop, set settings.menu-settings.shop-items. The in-game editor lists this setting as Product slot markers.

Add page buttons to an inventory shop

Repeat a single shop-items marker to make product slots. Use the layout markers as the button keys, just as with amount-items. For example, use previous-page-item.l and next-page-item.r. The layout below fits seven products per page.

The previous button hides on the first page and the next button hides on the final page by default. Use hidden-item to fill the slot, or leave it unset for an empty slot. Set the relevant hide option to false to keep the arrow visible at the boundary. Normal menu buttons stay in place as products change. Both page buttons are editable in the in-game editor.

Present the same shop in different interfaces — Premium

Inventory uses root size and layout.

Form and native Dialog turn selected entries into lists or actions.

Ore and ArcMenu use the selected style's ui.layout, ui.products.placement, and individual button ui.placement.

In a graphical shop, non-dynamic product collection can include products absent from root layout, using the default product placement. Dynamic layouts retain their selected products unless additional products have explicit graphical placement. Navigation controls always need their own placement. Use menu-visibility and display conditions to control eligibility.

UI Modes · Graphical UI Layout · Buy More

PreviousCommon Menus

NextBuy More Menus

Override one shop's template

Add products and navigation

Reuse a layout across shops

Add page buttons to an inventory shop

Present the same shop in different interfaces — Premium

Copy

settings:
shop-name: '&aFarming'
menu: farming
items:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&eWheat'
products:
1:
material: WHEAT
buy-prices:
1:
economy-plugin: Vault
amount: 5

Copy

settings:
shop-name: '&aFarming'
menu: farming
menu-settings:
title: '&aFarming Specials'
dialog:
enabled: true
layout: multi-action
bedrock:
enabled: true
content: '&7Browse farming products'
ui:
style: bedrock

Copy

size: 9
shop-items: ['P']
layout:
- '0PPPP000X'

Copy

size: 18
shop-items: ['P']
layout:
- '0PPPPPPP0'
- 'l0000000r'

previous-page-item:
l:
hide-on-first-page: true
display-item:
material: ARROW
name: '&ePrevious page'
hidden-item:
material: GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE
name: ' '

next-page-item:
r:
hide-on-last-page: true
display-item:
material: ARROW
name: '&eNext page'

🖥️Menu UI Modes

✨ArcMenu UI - Premium

ArcMenu displays Common, Shop, Search and Favourite menus as interactive graphical screens for Java players. It shares styles, areas, placements and product card settings with Ore, while using its own ArcMenu integration and client setup.

Enable ArcMenu

Install a compatible ArcMenu plugin and complete its client resource setup. This project integrates with the ArcMenu 3.4.3 API, including its native text input service.

In config.yml:

Copy

menu:
arcmenu:
enabled: true
ui:
style: classic

Save as menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
arcmenu:
enabled: true
content: '&7Choose a category'
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming
ui:
appearance: button
placement:
area: sidebar
order: 0

Create farming, reload and open /shop menu categories. The selected style provides the canvas and sidebar area. In normal selection, Java tries ArcMenu before an enabled Dialog/Ore route and Inventory. Bedrock uses its own presentation selection. See UI Modes for global forced selection.

Styles and layout

Use menu ui.style: classic or ui.style: bedrock, or select the global default with menu.ui.style. Style files live in plugins/UltimateShop/ui/, contain graphical fields directly, and can be copied for custom presets. Existing files are preserved when the plugin creates missing bundled presets.

Preset

Appearance and placement

classic

Vertical navigation at the left, product grid at the right

bedrock

Charcoal panels, beveled controls, green buying/hover states, pale selling controls, five-button top navigation and a three-column product grid

The bedrock preset uses the named sidebar area for its horizontal navigation. The name identifies an area, not a fixed screen position. Both presets use existing item icons and fonts. Adapt explicit row/column positions and custom areas when changing presets.

Menu-local fields override normal style defaults. A forced style can override local fields through menu.force-ui.style; see Configuration & Inheritance. Geometry and shared card keys are documented in Graphical UI Layout.

A button appears in the inventory but not in ArcMenu

ArcMenu skips ordinary buttons that have no ui.placement. The root layout sets inventory slots. Products can inherit ui.products.placement, while ordinary buttons need their own placement.

For example, the old blocks and blocks2 arrows used a shop_menu action to open a different shop. To make this kind of button appear in ArcMenu, add this under the existing button configuration:

If the button is defined in the shop file's buttons section, keep its marker in the shop menu's layout as well.

To browse more products in the same shop, use ArcMenu's own pagination. When the products fill more than one page, ui.layout.pagination positions the page controls. The shop menu's previous-page-item and next-page-item apply to inventories. See Graphical UI Layout for more placement options.

Product operations

Cards expose up to two available operations from ui.products.actions.primary, plus remaining or selected extra actions from ui.products.actions.extra. Operations must already exist in the configured product click bindings. Prices, conditions, quantity permissions and custom buy-only/sell-only restrictions still filter them.

Right-click defaults to select-amount. Change menu ui.renderers.arcmenu.right-click-action to select another available operation. select-amount opens graphical product details rather than rendering a Buy More file.

Default buy/sell quantities apply independently until the player selects a quantity. Plus/minus and scrolling adjust the selected quantity when Buy More and a quantity operation are available. Inventory amount-items, previews and confirmation slots do not define the graphical detail panel.

Item icons show the displayed stack count at their bottom-right when greater than one. This count describes the displayed stack, independently of the selected number of trade units.

Quantity input

With a compatible ArcMenu text input service, click the quantity between - and + to edit it directly. UltimateShop shows the live draft in the focused graphical field even when ArcMenu hides the native anvil screen.

Finish editing, using Esc in mouse mode, to apply a valid whole number. The value is clamped from 1 to the selected Buy More maximum; invalid input keeps the previous quantity. Finishing input only selects the amount. The player then chooses Buy or Sell to trade.

The input label is ui.text.amount-input. The source product still needs Buy More, a valid quantity menu and an available select-amount operation.

ArcMenu renderer settings

Menu-local renderer settings belong under ui.renderers.arcmenu:

Relative key

Purpose

right-click-action

Product right-click operation

screen-scale

Overall graphical scale

animation.enabled/duration

Opening animation; same-menu reopens skip it

more-menu.columns

Extra-action popup columns

tooltip.enabled/width/font-size/padding

Hover tooltip appearance

Refresh interval is read globally, even when other settings are local:

The interval is in ticks, with a minimum of 5. It can also be supplied by the selected global style. Per-menu update-interval does not set the refresh schedule.

Backgrounds, decorations and text

ArcMenu reads graphical colors from resolved ui.theme. Window and title backgrounds have independent enable switches. theme.decorations supports frames, corners and header accents; bevels and control-specific colors let a style define its appearance without depending on its file name. See the theme reference.

Tooltips wrap formatted lore in a left-aligned block and shrink oversized content to fit. Mouse layout fitting accounts for the active surface, cursor sensitivity, player preferences, cursor margin and the vertical pitch limit. Touch/Vision use the surface bounds.

Menu-type behavior

Search/Favourite use ArcMenu layouts and their own controls, while root result-slot capacity still limits source results. Favourite editing exposes reordering/removal controls. Item Sell/Sell All remain physical inventories. Buy More quantity selection stays within product details.

ArcMenu does not use /shop dialogpack for its own controls; that command builds Ore assets.

Search · Favourite · Buy More

PreviousOre UI - Premium

NextConditional Display - Premium

area: sidebar
order: 20

enabled: true

🖥️Menu UI Modes

🛏️Bedrock Form UI - Premium

Bedrock Form presents menu entries as a list, with separate product information and quantity input forms. It shares shop products, prices and trade rules with Java interfaces. It uses native Bedrock controls rather than graphical ui.layout coordinates.

Enable Form

A supported Geyser/Floodgate setup and Bedrock player detection are required. In config.yml:

Copy

menu:
bedrock:
enabled: true
check-method: FLOODGATE
not-auto-close: true
auto-add-icon:
enabled: true

In a Common menu, for example menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
bedrock:
enabled: true
content: '&7Choose a category'
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming

Create farming, reload, then open /shop menu categories as a Bedrock player. Normal selection requires both global and menu switches. Global menu.force-ui can override those switches; platform and integration requirements remain.

Entry list and visibility

Form collects root-layout entries and omits empty chest spacing. Shop product entries are listed before ordinary navigation controls. Buttons execute a LEFT click; a product normally opens its information Form.

Hide decorative entries with button/product menu-visibility.form: false. The active display item's bedrock.hide: true or an empty button name can also omit a Form button.

Icons and supplemental text

Place these fields inside the active display-item:

display-item.bedrock key

Meaning

icon

path;;<client texture path> or url;;<public image URL>

extra-line

Supplemental button text; products support price placeholders

hide

Hide this display variant from Form

path;; points into the Bedrock client's resources. url;; points to an image the client can load. Without an explicit icon, automatic icons attempt the vanilla material texture using bedrock.auto-add-icon. Java custom model data does not automatically supply a Bedrock texture. Put variant-specific icon options inside each conditional display item.

For products, an explicit display-item.bedrock.extra-line wins; '' intentionally leaves it blank. Otherwise, menu bedrock.price-extra-line falls back to global menu.bedrock.price-extra-line:

This text is independent of graphical ui.products.price.

Direct product actions

To run a product operation instead of opening information, add a product-root shortcut in the shop file:

Lookup order is product bedrock.click-event, owning shop menu bedrock.click-event, then global menu.bedrock.click-event. Only the LEFT product interaction uses this shortcut. Available bindings, trade conditions and item-aware selling routes remain in effect.

Form text reference

Supported options use menu bedrock.*, then global menu.bedrock.*. Player detection is global.

Relative key

Purpose

enabled

Allow Form

content

Menu description, with local root content fallback

check-method

Global detection method: FLOODGATE or UUID

not-auto-close

Reopen after supported information-form trades

auto-add-icon.enabled/format

Vanilla icon generation; format supports {version} and {path}

price-extra-line.default/only-buy/only-sell

Supplemental product price text

click-event

Default direct product operation

info.title, info.buttons.buy/sell/buy-more/sell-all/back

Product information text

buy-or-sell.title, buy-or-sell.buttons.amount.name/tip

Quantity input text

search.input, search.buttons.search

Search input and button

favourite-edit.title/content/buttons.*

Favourite editing text

{lang} uses the matching language override. Form product actions come from available click operations. Graphical ui.products.actions and Inventory Buy More confirmation labels do not define native Form controls.

Search and Favourite use dedicated Form flows. Item Sell and Sell All retain their physical inventories. The graphical ui.style: bedrock preset is a separate appearance setting for Ore/ArcMenu.

UI Modes · Buy More · Search · Favourite

Enable Form

Entry list and visibility

Icons and supplemental text

Direct product actions

Form text reference

Copy

buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
bedrock:
icon: 'path;;textures/items/wheat.png'
extra-line: '&7Seeds, crops and food'
hide: false

Copy

bedrock:
price-extra-line:
default: '&7Buy: {buy-price} | Sell: {sell-price}'
only-buy: '&7Buy: {buy-price}'
only-sell: '&7Sell: {sell-price}'

Copy

items:
A:
bedrock:
click-event: buy

🖥️Menu UI Modes

💬Native Dialog UI - Premium

Native Dialog uses supported Paper 1.21.9+ APIs for Java action buttons and item-action lists. These native layouts do not require Ore's generated resource pack. Ore is a separate graphical layout built on the Dialog route.

Enable native Dialog

In config.yml:

Copy

menu:
dialog:
enabled: true
not-auto-close: true
auto-add-sprite:
enabled: true
auto-hide: true
format: '<sprite:"{namespace}:{atlas}":{path}>'

Save this Common menu in menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
dialog:
enabled: true
layout: multi-action
content: '&7Choose a category'
button-width: 150
columns: 2
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming

Create farming, reload, then open /shop menu categories on a compatible Java client. Normal selection requires global/menu Dialog enablement. ArcMenu takes priority when enabled and available; global forced presentation selection overrides the normal switches.

Native layouts

Menu dialog.layout

Result

multi-action

Native action buttons

item-action-list

Native item-action list

ore

Graphical Ore for supported menu types

Root layout supplies source entries and order; empty Inventory slots do not produce blank Dialog rows. Individual entries can override native item-action behavior with their own dialog.layout. Visibility uses menu-visibility.dialog or menu-visibility.item-action-list for the active layout.

To suppress a material sprite on one button, add this to the button root:

dialog.show-sprite overrides automatic label-sprite selection. The global auto-add-sprite.auto-hide rule avoids duplicating label sprites when an item icon is already shown. These options belong outside display-item and outside graphical ui.

Dialog text and controls

Menu-relative key

Purpose

enabled

Allow Dialog

layout

Native or Ore selector

content

Body text; local root content fallback

button-width, columns

Menu-local native action dimensions; defaults 150 and 2

not-auto-close

Keep supported information actions open

default-button

Fallback exit label

info.display-item, info.title

Product information appearance

info.buttons.buy/sell/buy-more/sell-all/back

Information action labels

buy-more.display-item/title/input/buttons.confirm

Quantity input flow

search.input, search.buttons.search

Search controls

favourite-edit.title/content/buttons.*

Favourite editing controls

details

Ore product-details label

Supported shared text/behavior options fall back to global menu.dialog.*; content, button-width and columns are read from the menu itself. Automatic sprite settings are global under menu.dialog.auto-add-sprite. Labels support normal language references and placeholders.

Product and quantity flow

Selecting a product exposes available information actions. Operations are filtered by click bindings, prices and product eligibility. ui.products.actions configures graphical cards rather than native Dialog action buttons.

Quantity selection uses native input when the selected Buy More file enables Dialog and the product supports select-amount. Otherwise it uses the supported fallback. The source shop's dialog.buy-more text controls input labels; the Buy More file's Inventory amount buttons are not native widgets.

Until quantity selection, buy and sell use their independent default amounts. Selected quantities and per-opening menu/maximum overrides persist through native input and confirmation.

Search and Favourite

Search provides keyword input and result information. Favourite provides normal and editing controls. Neither converts Inventory physical deposit slots into native controls. For these menu types, dialog.layout: ore resolves to native multi-action.

Ore layout

Select menu dialog.layout: ore, then configure graphical areas and placements under ui. Common and Shop support Ore; it also requires the generated resource pack. Native button-width and columns do not position Ore cards.

UI Modes · Buy More · Search · Favourite

NextOre UI - Premium

show-sprite: false

🖥️Menu UI Modes

📦Inventory UI

Inventory displays chest-style slots on Java and Bedrock. It reads the menu's root size, layout and buttons. Graphical ui.layout and ui.placement do not change Inventory positions.

Select Inventory

For a single menu, disable alternative presentations at menu root:

Copy

dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false

For all menus, use config.yml:

Copy

menu:
force-ui:
enabled: true
type: inventory
style: ''

The global force setting takes priority over local switches. Disable it to restore normal selection. Inventory is also the standard fallback when a requested presentation cannot be used.

Configure rows and entries

Save as menus/common_menus/example.yml:

With Inventory selected, reload and open /shop menu example. Each row resolves to nine slots. Supported sizes are 9, 18, 27, 36, 45 and 54. Undefined IDs leave empty slots; 0 can be defined as a filler. Multi-character IDs use backticks. See Layout & Buttons.

Clicks, visibility and refresh

Ordinary buttons run actions after their click conditions pass. Shop products use configured product click operations. menu-visibility.inventory: false hides an entry only from Inventory; Premium display conditions can additionally select entries per player with dynamic layout.

Global refresh options in config.yml:

circle-update refreshes every second; click-update refreshes after a click. Enable periodic refresh when changing placeholders need it. Title updating is Premium and requires PacketEvents. Inventory click/reopen cooldowns are configured with menu.cooldown.click and menu.cooldown.reopen, in ticks; bundled values are -1.

Physical inventory screens

Item Sell accepts real items and requires sale confirmation. Sell All sells deposits on close. Both remain Inventory interfaces regardless of global presentation selection. Buy More has its own amount controls and product previews.

UI Modes · Display Item Add Lore

Copy

circle-update: false

title-update:
enabled: false
circle-update: false
click-update: true
resend-items-pack: false

🖥️Menu UI Modes

🪨Ore UI - Premium

Ore draws product cards and navigation controls through the Paper Dialog route and a generated resource pack. It supports Common and Shop menus on supported Paper 1.21.9+ servers. Graphical layout and styles are shared with ArcMenu.

Enable Ore

In config.yml:

Copy

menu:
dialog:
enabled: true
ore:
show-item-icon:
enabled: true
auto-hide-one: true
pack-format: 0
ui:
style: classic

Save as menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
dialog:
enabled: true
layout: ore
content: '&7Choose a category'
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming
ui:
appearance: button
placement:
area: sidebar
order: 0

Create the target shop, reload, generate the pack below, and load it on the Java client before opening /shop menu categories. This uses the selected style's sidebar area. Global forced presentation selection can override the switches.

Generate the resource pack

Run /shop reload after editing YAML so the menu definitions are loaded.

Run /shop dialogpack to build the enabled Ore menus. Permission: ultimateshop.dialogpack.

Use the generated output under plugins/UltimateShop/pack/ in the resource pack distributed to players.

Have clients load the updated pack before opening the changed screens.

/shop dialogpack <format> supplies an explicit pack format; accepted command values are 69 through 10000. Global menu.dialog.ore.pack-format: 0 selects the built-in version format. Rebuild and redistribute after changing graphical layout, theme, templates or icons. Reloading YAML alone does not distribute new client assets.

Pack generation includes Common/Shop menus using Ore, including a valid forced Ore selection. Search/Favourite are native Dialog fallbacks and do not generate Ore assets.

Layout and appearance

Key

Purpose

dialog.enabled, dialog.layout: ore

Select the route in normal selection

dialog.content

Header content, then local root content

ui.style

Shared graphical preset

ui.layout.canvas/header/areas/pagination

Canvas, areas, text and pagination

ui.products.placement

Default product placement

ui.products.card

Card icon/padding and Ore body lore limits

ui.products.price/actions

Product price text and operation selection

Entry ui.placement, ui.appearance

Individual button/product placement and button/card appearance

ui.theme

Shared colors

ui.renderers.ore.templates

Resource-pack texture templates

Global menu.dialog.ore.show-item-icon.*

Item icon and quantity display

Global menu.dialog.ore.pack-format

Pack metadata format

Root Inventory layout identifies entries but does not provide graphical coordinates. Ordinary controls need explicit placements. Product cards use ui.products.placement unless individually overridden. See Graphical UI Layout for geometry and capacity rules.

Custom template textures

Put PNG sources in plugins/UltimateShop/textures/, for example textures/shop/card.png, and configure:

Texture paths are relative to textures/; use neither absolute paths nor ... Quote RGB colors. Shared layout, card and theme values can supply generated template geometry/base colors. Regenerate the pack after edits.

Text and supported menu flows

Ore uses native text rendering. ArcMenu font scale settings do not imply identical Ore output. ui.products.card.lore-lines limits product body lore; description-lines limits category descriptions. Full applicable lore can remain in hover information.

Search/Favourite use native multi-action when Ore is selected. Buy More uses native quantity input or Inventory. Item Sell, Sell All and the in-game editor keep physical Inventory screens.

Native Dialog · ArcMenu · UI Modes

NextArcMenu UI - Premium

ui:

ore:
templates:
product:
texture: 'shop/card.png'
selected-texture: 'shop/card-selected.png'
selected-background: '#244A32'
selected-border: '#65D98B'

🖥️Menu UI Modes

🎨UI Modes

A presentation displays a menu using Inventory slots, native controls or graphical cards. Menu type determines which presentations are supported. Changing the presentation retains product prices, conditions and saved data.

Available presentations

Presentation

Client and requirements

Layout settings

Inventory

Java or Bedrock; standard menu interface

Root size, layout and buttons

Bedrock Form — Premium

Bedrock with a supported Geyser/Floodgate setup

bedrock.*; entries become a list

Native Dialog — Premium

Java on supported Paper 1.21.9+

dialog.layout: multi-action or item-action-list

Ore — Premium

Supported Paper Dialog runtime and generated client pack

dialog.layout: ore, plus graphical ui

ArcMenu — Premium

Java with a compatible ArcMenu installation and client setup

arcmenu.enabled, plus graphical ui

The native multi-action and item-action-list layouts do not require Ore's generated resource pack. ArcMenu follows its own integration's client requirements.

Normal presentation selection

Global switches in config.yml enable the available routes:

Copy

menu:
dialog:
enabled: true
bedrock:
enabled: true
arcmenu:
enabled: false

Individual menu files can narrow those choices:

For Java players, the opening order is ArcMenu → Dialog/Ore → Inventory. When ArcMenu cannot open, an enabled and supported Dialog route may still be used. The menu's dialog.layout selects the native layout or Ore.

Bedrock Form is checked separately for detected Bedrock players. To guarantee Inventory for both clients, use the forced Inventory setting below. Menu dialog.enabled and bedrock.enabled default to true; arcmenu.enabled defaults to false. Global enablement alone does not guarantee a compatible runtime or menu type.

Supported menu types

Menu type

Inventory

Bedrock Form

Native Dialog

Ore

ArcMenu

Common / Shop

Yes

Yes

Yes

Yes

Yes

Search / Favourite

Yes

Yes

Yes

Native multi-action fallback

Yes

Buy More

Dedicated slot controls

Source shop's quantity Form

Native quantity input

No separate graphical screen

Source screen's product details

Item Sell / Sell All

Yes

No

No

No

In-game editor

Yes

No

No

No

No

Search/Favourite with dialog.layout: ore use native multi-action and do not generate Ore assets. Buy More's Inventory layout is not rendered as a Form or graphical canvas. Item Sell/Sell All always provide physical Inventory slots, including when launched from another presentation.

Force one presentation

Use config.yml to choose a presentation across menus:

This overrides global and menu-local presentation enable switches. It preserves platform, edition, integration and menu-type requirements.

Forced type

Java

Bedrock

inventory

Inventory

Inventory

arcmenu

ArcMenu when available; otherwise Inventory

Inventory

dialog

Native Dialog when available

Inventory

item-action-list

Native item-action-list when supported

Inventory

ore

Ore for Common/Shop; native multi-action for Search/Favourite

Inventory

form

Inventory

Form when available

Unsupported routes retain their supported fallback. Item Sell/Sell All remain Inventory. Buy More may use native quantity input for a forced Dialog route; ArcMenu quantity controls remain part of product details. Forcing dialog replaces an Ore layout with multi-action; an existing native item-action-list selection remains native.

Set style to a style ID such as bedrock to force graphical defaults too, or leave it empty to retain normal style selection. See Configuration & Inheritance. An invalid type reports a configuration error and restores normal selection. Reload after changing the setting.

Configure layout, content and visibility

Change

Key

Inventory positions

Root size, layout

Native Dialog controls

dialog.*

Bedrock Form text and behavior

bedrock.*

Graphical style

Global menu.ui.style or menu ui.style

Ore/ArcMenu coordinates

ui.layout, entry ui.placement

Product card defaults

ui.products.*

Hide one entry in a presentation

Entry menu-visibility.<presentation>

Root content is the shared description fallback; dialog.content, bedrock.content and arcmenu.content override it for their route. The graphical ui section controls drawing and placement. It does not select a presentation with ui.mode.
